Abstract: We demonstrate X-DigCheck, a domain-independent environment for building and maintaining application profiles as they co-evolve with the data they describe. Profiles developed against a fixed ontology quickly drift from the schema they were meant to capture. X-DigCheck treats profile construction as a continuous ontology-data co-evolution loop: data are lifted into RDF against the profile, checked through competency questions and SHACL, and the resulting reports jointly drive revisions of the ontology, mappings, constraints, and graph. The loop is agnostic to the domain and to the pipeline that produces the graph. We validate and demonstrate the tool in the cultural heritage domain, on the construction of RupeMagna-RTI, the first Reflectance Transformation Imaging (RTI) specialisation of the Cultural Heritage Survey ODP (CHS-ODP), aligned with CIDOC-CRM/CRMdig, ArCo, CHAD-KG, and Getty AAT, with semRTI as the lifting pipeline of this use case. The demonstration lets visitors run one full turn of the loop -on the shipped Rupe Magna (Grosio, Italy) RTI survey, or on a profile and graph of their own -executing the competency-question and SHACL checks live and reading the bidirectional coverage report that flags modelling gaps and stale assumptions. The result is a portable co-evolution environment for profile engineering, together with a reusable RTI application profile produced through it. A screencast of the demonstration is available at https://zenodo.org/records/22210609.

</details>


### [142] [Preserving contextual information in cultural heritage metadata through multidimensional knowledge graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07695)
*Lyndon Nixon,Valentina Presutti,Celian Ringwald,Andrea Schimmenti*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出多维知识图谱（MKGs）概念，以解决标准知识图谱无法处理文化遗产元数据中语境依赖性和多维度冲突的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识图谱难以捕捉陈述的语境依赖性有效性，而文化遗产元数据需容纳演变或冲突的观点（如殖民与后殖民视角、科学共识变化），现有方法缺乏统一框架来同时建模和查询跨社会、文化及政治维度的数据。

Method: 引入多维知识图谱（MKGs）的概念基础，讨论其如何在文化遗产元数据中保留复杂的多层语境。

Result: 构建了MKGs的理论框架，能够同时建模和查询涉及多个社会、文化和政治维度的复杂语境数据。

Conclusion: MKGs为文化遗产元数据提供了处理多层面语境和观点冲突的统一解决方案，提升了语义丰富性和互操作性。

Abstract: Using Knowledge Graphs (KGs) to describe Cultural Heritage Objects (CHOs) supports semantic richness and interoperability. However, standard KGs fail to capture the context-dependent validity of statements. This limitation is critical for cultural heritage metadata, which must often accommodate evolving or conflicting viewpoints, such as colonial versus post-colonial perspectives or shifting scientific consensus. While current knowledge representation methods address basic contextualization via provenance, qualifiers or reification, they lack a unified framework to simultaneously model and query data across multiple social, cultural, and political dimensions. To bridge this gap, we introduce the conceptual foundations of Multi-dimensional Knowledge Graphs (MKGs) and discuss how they preserve complex, multi-layered contexts in CHO metadata.

</details>


### [143] [TrajectoryDB: A New Database for Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07782)
*Yunjia Zheng,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajectoryDB，一种专为AI代理执行轨迹设计的原生数据管理系统，旨在解决当前轨迹数据分散、缺乏持久化管理的问题，通过协同设计摄入、存储和查询处理流程，高效管理具有层级结构、语义信息和依赖关系的复杂轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理生成的执行轨迹对于记忆提取、模型微调、运行时优化及安全监控等下游任务至关重要。然而，当前的轨迹数据分散在文件、数据库和可观测性系统中，缺乏针对其独特结构和访问模式设计的持久化数据管理系统。

Method: 将轨迹视为一种独立的数据类型，提出TrajectoryDB系统。该系统协同设计数据摄入（重建并保留执行结构和谱系）、存储（高效组织高冗余上下文并保持记录间关系）和查询处理（联合推理结构、时间顺序、语义和谱系），以实现对代理执行轨迹的高效管理和分析。

Result: 确立了轨迹作为独立数据类型的必要性，并提出了一个能够处理层级执行结构、大规模文本语义推理以及事件与状态间丰富依赖关系的新型数据管理架构。

Conclusion: 轨迹数据因其独特的结构特性需要专门的管理系统。TrajectoryDB通过端到端的协同设计，为AI代理执行轨迹提供了统一、高效的存储和分析解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents generate rich execution trajectories that capture their interactions with large language models, tools, and external environments. These trajectories are increasingly valuable for downstream tasks such as memory extraction, model fine-tuning, runtime optimization, and security and cost monitoring. Yet trajectory data today is fragmented across files, databases, and observability systems, with no persistent data management system designed around its unique structure and access patterns.
  We argue that trajectories should be treated as a distinct data type. A trajectory combines hierarchical execution structure, large volumes of text whose analysis often requires semantic reasoning, and rich dependencies and lineage among events, intermediate states, and derived artifacts. These properties introduce new requirements throughout the data lifecycle. Ingestion must reconstruct and preserve execution structure and lineage; storage must efficiently organize large but highly redundant contexts while maintaining relationships among records; and query processing must jointly reason over structure, temporal order, semantics, and lineage.
  We therefore envision TrajectoryDB, a trajectory-native data management system that co-designs ingestion, storage, and query processing to efficiently manage and analyze agent execution trajectories.

</details>


### [144] [Generalized DBLog: A Verified Contract for Interleaving Database Rows with a Change Log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08160)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文针对CDC系统中的“复制到日志交接”问题，通过形式化验证方法，证明了DBLog及其变体（如Debezium、Flink CDC）在不同读取策略下，能够保证数据一致性和正确性，无需单一全局快照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CDC管道在启动、添加表或修复数据时，将存量数据复制与实时日志流合并过程中出现的“复制到日志交接”问题，确保数据不丢失、不重复、不覆盖，并验证现有工程实现（如Netflix DBLog, Debezium, Flink CDC）的理论正确性。

Method: 提出Generalized DBLog理论框架，明确源数据和捕获实现需满足的条件；使用Isabelle/HOL进行机器检查，Lean 4独立验证核心逻辑，并通过TLA+进行有界模型检查，对多种CDC变体（经典水印、信号表、并行分块等）进行形式化证明。

Result: 证明了在所有选定表和键范围内，即使行以不同时间读取，只要完成复制和协调过程，重建的状态即与源数据库一致；后续日志变更可逐事件推进状态；该保证适用于经典水印、Debezium信号表/只读模式、Flink CDC并行分块等多种场景。

Conclusion: 无需单一数据库快照即可保证CDC数据一致性；提出的通用条件和方法为各类CDC引擎提供了坚实的理论基础，并通过多重形式化工具验证了其协议的正确性。

Abstract: Change-data capture (CDC) feeds downstream systems like caches, search indexes, and data warehouses from a database's log of committed row changes. When bootstrapping, adding a table, or repairing downstream data, a pipeline must also copy existing rows. Merging this copy with the active log introduces the copy-to-log handoff problem. Changes must not fall through a gap, and older copied state must not overwrite a newer logged update or resurrect a deleted row. DBLog, developed at Netflix, addressed this problem by reading tables in chunks and interleaving those reads with the live log. Watermarks identify the changes that overlap each read, and the log wins when a copied row is stale. Debezium and Flink CDC have since adapted this design. Earlier work proved that applying the original algorithm's copied rows and logged changes in their emitted order reconstructs the source's rows, including the effect of every logged insert, update, and delete processed. Generalized DBLog asks when the same result holds for variants of that design. We state the conditions the source and capture implementation must satisfy. Once copying and reconciliation are complete, we prove that the result holds across all selected tables and key ranges even when their rows were read at different times. A single database snapshot is not required for the copy. Further logged changes advance the reconstructed state one event at a time. We establish these guarantees for classic watermarking, Debezium's signal-table and read-only modes, Flink CDC's parallel chunks, reads and dumps tied to exact log positions, and engine-consistent backups whose log position lies within known bounds. The complete theory is machine-checked in Isabelle/HOL, its core independently verified in Lean 4, and the protocols are also examined by bounded model checking in TLA+.

</details>


### [145] [UnespDataLens-RM: A Reference Model for Analytical Data Engineering with Governance, Quality, Provenance, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08184)
*Ronaldo Celso Messias Correia,Douglas Francisquini Toledo,Camila Tolin Santos da Silva*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出UnespDataLens-RM参考模型，整合分析数据工程中的技术操作与跨领域能力（治理、质量、溯源等），解决方法论碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析依赖日益增强，但现有方法在治理、质量保证、溯源、版本控制和可重复性等方面存在方法论碎片化，导致分析资产可靠性不足。

Method: 基于设计科学研究方法，构建包含8个技术操作模块、8个跨领域模块及正式化工件/指标/验证标准的无技术依赖性参考模型。

Result: 确立了概念与方法论框架，支持分析管道的规范、组织和演化，使其具备更强的可治理性、文档化、可追溯性、可审计性和可重复性。

Conclusion: 该模型为未来分析管道的设计提供了统一结构，有助于提升数据生命周期管理的一致性与可靠性。

Abstract: The growing reliance on data in analytical processes and evidence-based decision-making has reinforced the importance of Data Engineering in building pipelines capable of integrating, transforming, validating, and delivering data from heterogeneous sources. However, the reliability of analytical assets depends not only on data processing capabilities but also on mechanisms for governance, quality assurance, provenance, traceability, versioning, and reproducibility throughout their lifecycle. These responsibilities are commonly addressed by different models, frameworks, and operational practices, resulting in methodological fragmentation across the analytical data lifecycle. To address this gap, this article proposes UnespDataLens-RM, a technology-independent reference model that integrates technical-operational processes and cross-cutting capabilities within a unified structure for Analytical Data Engineering. The model aims to support the specification, organization, and evolution of analytical pipelines by incorporating governance, quality, provenance, traceability, and reproducibility from the design stage. Developed following the Design Science Research approach, UnespDataLens-RM comprises eight technical-operational modules, eight cross-cutting modules, complementary dimensions, and a formalized set of artifacts, metrics, and validation criteria. The resulting specification offers a conceptual and methodological framework for future instantiations and empirical evaluations of analytical pipelines designed to be more governable, documented, traceable, auditable, and reproducible.

</details>


### [146] [SmartANN: Object Causal Modeling Boosts Approximate Nearest Neighbor Diagnosis and Auto-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08240)
*Yutong Zhou,Guoxin Kang,Lei Wang,Xueya Zhang,Qinwei Yang,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出SmartANN框架，基于对象因果模型（OCM）对近似最近邻（ANN）算法进行瓶颈归因和自动重设计。通过顺序诊断-替换循环识别上游瓶颈并优化，显著提升IVF-PQ和HNSW等算法的召回率（提升0.24%-74.20%）和查询速度（QPS提升28.8%-256.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANN研究多关注孤立的设计选择比较或整体指标报告，缺乏对索引构建与查询执行之间性能损失传播机制的追踪，导致难以准确定位根本原因及实现自动化重设计。

Method: 将ANN工作流表示为八个有序、可替换的对象；采用“诊断-替换”循环：识别首个偏离预期行为的对象作为瓶颈，若存在测试预言机则替换为它，否则替换为表现更好的实现，随后继续下游诊断；从可插拔动作库中组合兼容动作以生成端到端优化设计。

Result: 在八个真实数据集上验证，相比基线，SmartANN在保持相似召回率下，Recall提升0.24%-74.20%，QPS提升28.8%-256.5%，且诊断与自动设计开销较低。

Conclusion: SmartANN有效解决了ANN中跨阶段性能损失传播导致的归因难题，实现了高效的自动化重设计，适用于分区-量化类（如IVF-PQ）和基于图类（如HNSW）的ANN算法。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) algorithms achieve high efficiency through interdependent phases across index construction and query execution. This coupling allows upstream performance loss to propagate downstream, affecting execution behavior and measurable outputs. Existing component-level analyses mainly compare isolated design choices, while end-to-end benchmarks report aggregate metrics; neither traces loss propagation across dependent phases, hindering root-cause attribution and automated redesign.
  We present SmartANN, a framework based on the object causal model (OCM) for ANN bottleneck attribution and automated redesign. SmartANN represents an ANN workflow as eight ordered, replaceable objects and diagnoses them with a sequential diagnose-and-replace loop. At each iteration, it identifies the first object deviating from expected behavior or output as a bottleneck. Because an upstream bottleneck can obscure downstream ones, SmartANN replaces it with a test oracle when available, or with an implementation producing a better outcome, then continues downstream diagnosis. From the diagnosed bottlenecks and failure causes, SmartANN composes compatible actions from a pluggable action library to generate an optimized end-to-end ANN design. We instantiate SmartANN for IVF-PQ and HNSW, covering partition-and-quantization and graph-based ANN families.
  Experiments on eight real-world datasets show that SmartANN improves Recall by 0.24--74.20%, and increases QPS by 28.8--256.5% at comparable Recall, with low diagnosis and auto-design overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/zhouyutong20/SmartANN.

</details>


### [147] [WiDiff: Extracting Changes from Wikidata's Edit History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08508)
*Carolina Cortés,Lisa Ehrlinger,Lorena Etcheverry,Felix Naumann*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WiDiff工具，用于从Wikidata的完整编辑历史中提取变更并提供统一的大规模分析查询接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解知识图谱的演变对于确保下游应用（如问答、实体链接）的可靠性至关重要，而现有的Wikidata数据格式不支持对完整编辑历史的分析查询。

Method: 开发WiDiff工具，从Wikidata的完整编辑历史中提取变更数据，并构建统一的接口以支持大规模分析查询。

Result: 成功实现了能够处理超过1.2亿实体和十年以上编辑历史的变更提取与分析功能。

Conclusion: WiDiff填补了现有工具在分析Wikidata完整编辑历史方面的空白，为研究知识图谱演变提供了必要的基础设施。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have become a key resource for integrating heterogeneous data and powering downstream tasks such as question answering, entity linking, and semantic search. They are built and maintained incrementally, either (i) fully automated, e.g., YAGO, (ii) semiautomatically with community oversight, e.g., DBpedia, or (iii) manually through collaborative editing, e.g., Wikidata. Understanding the evolution of knowledge graphs is essential as changes may reflect real-world updates, error corrections, or noise introduced by vandalism, all of which affect the reliability of downstream applications. Among openly available knowledge graphs, Wikidata is the most challenging case to study evolution, with over 120 million entities edited by humans and bots and an edit history spanning more than a decade. Although Wikidata exposes change data in various formats (e.g., periodic dumps and real-time event streams), none support analytical queries over the complete edit history. Therefore, we present WiDiff, a tool that extracts changes from Wikidata's complete edit history and provides a unified interface for large-scale analytical queries over it.

</details>


### [148] [SQLMorph: Query Mutation and Fine-Grained Metrics for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08950)
*Mohammadhossein Malekpour,Mohamed Riahi,Maxime Lamothe,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出SQLMorph框架，通过查询变异（JQE和TQA）自动扩展Text-to-SQL评估集，并引入执行级细粒度指标（EXP、EXR、F1），以解决现有基准无法反映企业复杂性且评估结果难以复现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL评估存在瓶颈：公开基准无法捕捉企业模式的复杂性，而构建私有评估集成本高且非确定性，导致结果难以复现。

Method: 1. 查询变异技术：Join Query Expansion (JQE) 通过有效连接增加结构复杂性；Textual Query Augmentation (TQA) 生成受控的自然语言扰动以评估对语言变化的鲁棒性。2. 细粒度执行指标：定义执行精度(EXP)和执行召回率(EXR)，并结合为F1分数，替代传统的二进制准确率。

Result: JQE揭示了随着连接数增加准确率下降的趋势；TQA显示重度缩写导致的语言脆弱性可使准确率降低高达17%。新指标实现了过度预测和不足预测的细粒度分析，揭示了二进制指标所掩盖的系统差异。

Conclusion: SQLMorph通过查询变异和细粒度指标支持系统调试，使Text-to-SQL评估实践更贴近真实世界部署需求。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems translate natural language queries into executable SQL, democratizing access to structured data. Despite recent advances driven by large language models (LLMs), evaluation remains a major bottleneck: public benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of enterprise schema, while building private evaluation sets is costly and nondeterministic, making evaluation results difficult to reproduce. To address this issue, we present SQLMorph, a framework for Text-to-SQL evaluation via query mutation. SQLMorph introduces two techniques to automatically generate and expand evaluation sets: Join Query Expansion (JQE), which systematically increases structural complexity through valid join additions, and Textual Query Augmentation (TQA), which generates controlled natural language perturbations to assess robustness to linguistic variation. JQE and TQA create targeted choke points to challenge specific system components. When applied to state-of-the-art systems, JQE increases query coverage and reveals accuracy degradation as the number of joins grows. Meanwhile, TQA shows that linguistic brittleness induced by heavy abbreviation can reduce accuracy by up to 17%.
  Beyond evaluation sets, SQLMorph introduces a family of execution-level metrics that address the limitations of current binary measures, such as Execution Accuracy. We define Execution Precision (EXP) and Execution Recall (EXR) to quantify the fraction of correct and recovered results, respectively, and combine them via F1 for unified scoring. Our experiments show that these relaxed metrics enable fine-grained analysis of over- and under-prediction, revealing differences across systems that binary metrics obscure. Together, SQLMorph's query mutation and fine-grained metrics support debugging and better align Text-to-SQL evaluation practices with real-world deployments.

</details>


### [149] [Factorized and Vectorized Execution: Optimizing Analytical and Semantic Queries over Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09002)
*Sunny Yasser,Anas Dorbani,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: FFX是一个支持任意因子化方案并保持完全向量化执行的新型引擎，通过打包因子化向量和缓存友好的布局解决中间结果爆炸问题，同时优化LLM语义算子以降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对多连接在分析性和语义工作负载中至关重要，但常导致中间结果爆炸；现有方法要么依赖完全物化，要么仅支持受限的因子化布局，无法兼顾因子化的紧凑性与向量化执行的高效性。

Method: 提出FFX引擎，首次实现管道式执行以支持任意因子化方案并保留全向量化特性；引入打包因子化向量及操作符以维持缓存友好的连续布局；此外，通过将因子化中间结果序列化为LLM提示词来协同优化语义算子。

Result: FFX能够高效执行包含大量连接的查询以及增强语义算子的查询，显著减少LLM的token使用量和推理成本，同时保持或提升输出质量。

Conclusion: FFX成功结合了因子化表示的紧凑性和向量化执行的性能优势，为处理复杂连接和语义任务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Many-to-many joins are central to analytical and semantic workloads such as fraud detection, network analysis, and recommendation, where insights arise from relationships between entities. These workloads often suffer from an explosion of intermediate results, sometimes orders of magnitude larger than the inputs. Factorized representations address this problem by exploiting conditional independence among attributes to encode intermediates more compactly. In some cases, they can reduce the output size asymptotically below the worst-case output size. However, adopting factorization in modern vectorized query processors remains challenging: factorized representations are hierarchical, whereas vectorized execution is built around flat, block-oriented processing. Prior approaches either rely on full materialization or support only restricted factorization layouts, sacrificing much of the benefits of both factorization and vectorization.
  We present FFX, a novel engine for Fast Factorized eXecution. FFX is the first pipelined engine to support arbitrary factorization schemes while preserving full vectorization. The engine introduces packed factorized vectors and operators that maintain cache-friendly, contiguous layouts. Beyond analytics, FFX also co-optimizes semantic operators by serializing factorized intermediates into compact prompts for large language models (LLMs), substantially reducing token usage and inference cost while maintaining output quality and, in some cases, improving it. Together, these contributions enable efficient execution of join-heavy analytical queries, including queries augmented with semantic operators.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Beyond Right and Wrong: Evaluating Second-order Social Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05437)
*Sunny Rai,Jinyi Kuang,Reyhan Jamalova,Annie Lou,Cristina Bicchieri,Niyati Malhotra,Victor Hugo Orozco-Olvera,Ana Maria Munoz-Boudet,Lyle H Ungar,Sharath C Guntuku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出评估大语言模型（LLM）“元规范”推理能力的新框架，揭示当前AI倾向于过度预测惩罚而忽视现实中的宽容与关系调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究多关注一阶社会规范（如“不可盗窃”），但缺乏对社会智能中更复杂的二阶预期（即元规范：人们如何回应违规者及观察者反应）的考量。

Method: 引入基于情感评估和行为响应的双维度评估框架；发布包含450个场景的手动标注数据集NormReact，涵盖违规者性别与观察者社交亲疏等多视角数据；设计预测违规者自我调节和观察者他律调节的分类任务。

Result: 测试显示，六个主流LLM模型描绘了一个比人类预期更严厉的社会图景：在人类预期不作为时过度预测负面制裁，且随着社交距离增加，其与人类判断的一致性显著下降。

Conclusion: AI系统在冲突调解或政策模拟等敏感领域可能产生扭曲的社会监管认知，过度强调惩罚而低估现实世界中普遍存在的宽容、克制及关系校准机制。

Abstract: Previous AI alignment efforts have focused primarily on first-order social norms -- teaching models what is socially acceptable or unacceptable (e.g., `do not steal'). However, social intelligence depends not only on norm recognition, but also on anticipating who will enforce it and how (e.g., public shame or even imprisonment). These second-order expectations, known as metanorms, govern how people respond when social rules are broken. We introduce a novel framework for evaluating metanorm reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) along two dimensions: emotional appraisal and behavioral response, and propose new classification tasks, namely, predicting self-regulation in violators, and other-regulation in observers. We release a multi-perspective dataset, NormReact, of 450 norm violation scenarios, hand-annotated for emotions and behavioral responses across norm violators' gender and observers' social closeness. Current LLMs portray a harsher social world: across six models, they overpredict negative sanctions where humans would expect inaction, and alignment with human judgments deteriorates as social distance increases. These findings suggest that AI systems in norm-sensitive domains from conflict mediation to policy simulation, may risk producing a distorted picture of social regulation: one that over-represents punishment and under-represents the tolerance, restraint, and relational calibration that characterize actual norm enforcement in real world.

</details>


### [151] [CriticGen: Generation-Aware Evaluation as Actionable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05439)
*Huifang Du,Zecheng Zuo,Sen Wang,Chenghao Fan,Haofen Wang,Yehui Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CriticGen框架，通过细粒度、生成感知的评估维度，将LLM评估转化为可执行的反馈，显著提升答案改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估方法过于粗糙且与生成过程脱节，产生的通用解释无法为模型改进提供可操作的反馈。

Method: 提出CriticGen框架：1) 基于高层类别（主观、客观等）生成样本特定的评估维度和评分标准；2) 利用动态评分表联合生成分数、理由、可执行优化建议及优化后的答案；3) 实现诊断缺陷与针对性改进的闭环。

Result: 1) 评估相关性/覆盖率提升至3.97/4.24；2) 得分相关性达到Pearson 0.9556和Spearman 0.9560；3) 基于标准的理由和建议F1值分别提升至0.7554和0.7900；4) 成功改进73.17%的答案，非退化率达93.28%。

Conclusion: 细粒度评估应具备实例特定性和可操作性。CriticGen能有效生成高质量评分标准，并通过可靠的反馈机制实现显著的答案质量提升。

Abstract: Current evaluation methods for large language models are coarse-grained and decoupled from generation, producing generic explanations that fail to provide actionable feedback for model improvement. We propose CriticGen, a fine-grained, generation-aware evaluation framework that turns evaluation into actionable control for answer improvement. CriticGen first generates sample-specific evaluation dimensions and scoring criteria under high-level categories such as subjective, objective, and self-derived constraints. These criteria then serve as a dynamic rubric for jointly producing a score, a reason, an executable refinement suggestion, and a refined answer. This rubric-conditioned refinement process enables models to diagnose flaws and perform targeted answer improvement. Experimental results show that fine-grained evaluation should be both instance-specific and actionable. CriticGen induces higher-quality rubrics, improving relevance/coverage from 3.33/4.03 to 3.97/4.24. CriticGen also achieves the best score correlations, with 0.9556 Pearson and 0.9560 Spearman, and raises the F1 of criterion-grounded reasons and executable suggestions from 0.6369/0.5994 to 0.7554/0.7900. Crucially, its feedback translates into reliable answer improvement, improving 73.17% of answers with a 93.28% non-degradation rate.

</details>


### [152] [When Does Memory Help? A Cost-Aware Evaluation of Long-Term Memory in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05441)
*Shweta Mishra,Shashank Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MERIT基准，通过显式成本核算评估LLM代理在工具使用任务中的记忆边际效用，发现结构化存储优于嵌入检索，且最优配置的经济效益最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如LoCoMo、LongMemEval）仅衡量对话历史中的问答能力，无法反映记忆是否真正改变了工具使用代理的行为。

Method: 构建MERIT基准与测试框架，包含三个领域的 episodic 工具使用任务；采用自动化泄漏检查验证事实依赖性；设置难度阶梯、受控记忆腐败及全代币/美元计量；进行多模型、多种子网格实验及最新模型复现。

Result: 记忆将依赖任务成功率从0提升至0.55-1.00；嵌入检索在处理更新事实时表现不稳定（0.30-0.95），代理仅55%时间基于正确检索值行动；更新即写存储（结构化事实库和LLM摘要）保持0.70-1.00的高成功率，混合模式效果不如单一结构化存储；记忆实现方式改变可导致任务成功率波动高达60个百分点。

Conclusion: 不同记忆实现方案对任务成功率影响巨大，全回放策略不具备经济性，各领域最佳条件的每美元边际效用是其2.7-3.9倍；已开源基准、框架及所有追踪数据。

Abstract: Long-term memory for LLM agents is evaluated today by conversational recall benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval), which measure question answering over dialogue history, not whether remembered facts change what a tool-using agent does. We present MERIT (Memory Evaluation for Realistic Instrumented Tasks), a benchmark and harness that measures the marginal utility of memory for task-executing agents under explicit cost accounting. MERIT provides episodic tool-use tasks in three domains whose dependence on earlier-episode facts is verified by an automated leak check; a difficulty ladder ending in updated-fact recall; controlled memory corruption; and full token and dollar metering of every memory operation. Across 23,440 scored episodes ($42.57), a two-generation pilot on gpt-4.1-mini and a preregistered 3-model x 3-seed grid (GPT-4.1, Claude Haiku 4.5; memory side held fixed), memory lifts dependent-task success from a leak-verified floor of 0.00 to 0.55-1.00. On updated facts, embedding retrieval collapses unpredictably (0.30-0.95 across models; max seed gap 0.45), and agents act on a correctly retrieved value only 55% of the time, while update-on-write stores (a structured fact store and, notably, LLM summarization) remain at 0.70-1.00; the hybrid is worse than the fact store alone. A latest-generation spot-check (Claude Sonnet 5, gated on a clean full-replay control) reproduces the pattern. Swapping a memory's implementation moves task success by up to 60 points, and full replay is never economical: the best condition per domain delivers 2.7-3.9x its marginal utility per dollar. We release the benchmark, harness, and all traces.

</details>


### [153] [AutoFyn Technical Report: Non-Parametric Expert Iteration for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05446)
*Adib Hasan,Daniel Schaffield,Akashnil Dutta,Tarik Adnan Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFyn是一种基于Expert Iteration算法的AI智能体框架，通过更新持久化状态（而非模型权重）来迭代优化。它在奥林匹克数学、数据科学和网络安全领域表现出色，显著提升了模型在复杂任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统强化学习中频繁更新模型权重带来的计算成本高和稳定性差的问题，提出一种仅通过更新持久化状态即可实现智能体持续进化的新范式。

Method: 引入AutoFyn框架，利用冻结的基础模型，在多轮迭代中通过验证器提供的奖励信号更新持久化状态（如记忆文件、报告、仓库状态）。每轮开始时重置模型会话，仅通过显式接口 reintroduce 持久信息。包含探索、规划、构建的协调器和任务特定的验证器。

Result: 在2026年国际数学奥林匹克竞赛（IMO）的6道新题上，使用AutoFyn的模型得分均高于其原生编程智能体；在Spider 2.0 dbt基准测试中构建了排名第一的智能体；并在Next.js、MetaMask等多个项目中产生了16个经维护者确认的安全漏洞公告。

Conclusion: AutoFyn通过分离模型权重与策略状态，有效实现了智能体的低成本、高稳定性迭代优化，证明了其在数学推理、数据工程和网络安全等高难度领域具有显著优势。

Abstract: We introduce AutoFyn, an agent harness inspired by the Expert Iteration algorithm, adapting a frozen model across many rounds by updating persistent state from verified reward signals rather than model weights. Each round begins from a fresh model session, and durable information is reintroduced only through explicit interfaces such as persistent memory files, reports, and repository state. Within a round, an orchestrator explores, plans and builds many alternative approaches with specialized agents, while a task-grounded verifier verifies the work and supplies an objective reward for measuring progress. This reward is distilled back into the persistent state, which updates the effective policy for the next round. In this technical report, we formalize this loop and describe its persistent state and verification interfaces. We then demonstrate its use in three domains, namely olympiad mathematics, data science, and cybersecurity. On the six fresh problems of the 2026 International Mathematical Olympiad, every model with room to improve scores higher under AutoFyn than in its provider's own coding agent. AutoFyn also built the top-ranked agent on the Spider 2.0 dbt benchmark, and has produced $16$ maintainer-confirmed vulnerability advisories in Next.js, MetaMask, pnpm, Warp, LiteLLM, Langflow, and Open WebUI.

</details>


### [154] [Damage-Aware Bandit Pruning for Vision and Language Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05448)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于损伤感知多臂老虎机（Damage-aware Multi-armed Bandit）的Transformer结构化后训练剪枝方法，通过Ucb或Fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling策略选择需抑制的功能单元（Attention heads/MLP channels），在多个语言模型和视觉模型上证明了其相比基线方法能更有效地减少性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化后训练剪枝需要选择完整的功能单元进行抑制，同时确保由此造成的性能退化最小化。现有方法在预算受限下难以平衡评估成本与剪枝效果。

Method: 将结构化单元选择建模为固定候选评估预算下的损伤感知多臂老虎机问题。具体步骤包括：1) 在校准批次上临时掩码Attention heads和MLP通道组；2) 计算成对损伤（Paired damage = 掩码损失 - 基础损失）以减少批次间波动；3) 使用平滑有界奖励驱动UCB风格策略或Fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling；4) 逐步构建最终掩码，将选中的单元在原始稠密检查点中功能归零。

Result: 在WikiText-2, LAMBADA, Imagenette数据集上的GPT-2, OPT, Pythia, Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, ViT-B/16, DeiT-Tiny, Swin-Tiny等模型上进行实验。结果表明：1) 与随机、幅度、静态显著性、预算贪心等方法相比，老虎机方法通常能减少性能退化；2) 统计显著性分析显示，28个对比中有23个置信区间排除零，11个配对检验p < 0.05，6个经过Benjamini-Hochberg校正后q < 0.05；3) 视觉模型的增益不能仅由更大的评估预算解释。

Conclusion: 提出的损伤感知多臂老虎机方法能有效指导Transformer的结构化剪枝，在有限评估预算下优于传统选择策略，实现了有效的结构性抑制而非物理压缩。

Abstract: Structured post-training pruning of transformers requires selecting complete functional units whose suppression causes limited degradation. We formulate structured-unit selection for language and vision transformers as a damage-aware multi-armed bandit problem under a fixed candidate-evaluation budget. Attention heads and MLP channel groups are temporarily masked on calibration batches. Paired damage is the masked loss minus the base loss on the same batch, reducing batch-to-batch variation. A smooth bounded reward drives either a UCB-style policy or fractional-Beta Thompson Sampling, and the final mask is constructed sequentially by adding one unit at each step. The selected units are functionally zeroed in the original dense checkpoint; therefore, the reported parameter effects represent effective structural suppression rather than physical compression or measured speedup. Experiments on WikiText-2, LAMBADA, and Imagenette cover GPT-2, OPT, Pythia, Qwen2.5, SmolLM2, ViT-B/16, DeiT-Tiny, and Swin-Tiny, with comparisons against random, magnitude, static-saliency, and budgeted-greedy selection. Across five seeds, the bandit methods usually reduce degradation relative to budgeted greedy in the paired language-model comparisons. Of 28 comparisons highlighted in the paper, 23 bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero and 11 paired tests have p < 0.05; six have q < 0.05 after Benjamini-Hochberg correction across the full family of 116 dataset-wise tests. Matched-evaluation results for ViT-B/16 and Swin-Tiny indicate that their gains are not explained solely by a larger candidate-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [155] [Compiling VGDL into Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05459)
*Mohit Jiwatode,Bodo Rosenhahn,Alexander Dockhorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性框架，将视频游戏描述语言（VGDL）直接编译为动态结构因果模型（DSCM），以解决强化学习和大语言模型在游戏环境因果机制捕捉上的不足，确保绝对因果保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习代理依赖虚假相关，大语言模型易产生幻觉，且缺乏将复杂游戏机制直接映射到因果模型的正式方法，导致难以准确捕捉游戏环境的因果力学。

Method: 构建确定性框架，直接将VGDL指定的游戏组件（如精灵动力学、交互规则、终止条件）转化为显式结构方程。每个游戏刻（tick）表示从时间t到t+1的状态变量因果转换，而非从游戏轨迹或LLM输出中推断因果结构。

Result: 建立了游戏符号描述与因果 grounded AI 之间的原则性桥梁，生成的模型提供透明的因果路径，保证了对真实游戏机械结构的绝对因果保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架支持反事实推理、因果强化学习智能体训练及程序化内容验证，为基于因果的游戏人工智能提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and large language models often struggle to accurately capture the causal mechanics of game environments. Standard reinforcement learning agents tend to rely on spurious correlations, while large language models are prone to hallucinating game rules. Although causal reinforcement learning improves interpretability, there is currently no formal methodology to map complex game mechanics directly into causal models. To address this, we propose a deterministic framework that compiles games specified in the Video Game Description Language into Dynamic Structural Causal Models. Rather than inferring causal structures from gameplay traces or noisy large language models' outputs, our methodology directly translates game components, including sprite dynamics, interaction rules, and termination conditions, into explicit structural equations. Each game tick represents a causal transition from state variables at time $t$ to $t+1$. By establishing this grounded mapping, the approach guarantees absolute causal fidelity to the ground-truth game mechanics. The resulting models offer transparent causal pathways that support counterfactual reasoning, causal reinforcement learning agent training, and procedural content validation. This framework provides a principled bridge between symbolic game descriptions and causally grounded game AI.

</details>


### [156] [ARC-Bench: Closed-Loop Replanning Masks Broken Action Ranking in Frozen JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05461)
*Zhengshu Zhang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过ARC-Bench基准测试，揭示了JEPA风格无奖励潜在世界模型中“潜在空间距离可排序候选动作”这一假设存在严重且结构性的失效。尽管闭环重规划掩盖了这一问题，导致成功率虚高，但直接审计表明冻结的潜在表示无法正确对动作进行优劣排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估无奖励潜在世界模型（如JEPA-WM）的核心假设是否成立：即预测的未来嵌入与目标嵌入之间的潜在空间距离，是否能准确反映并排序真实动作成本。此前该假设未被直接审计，其有效性存疑。

Method: 引入ARC-Bench，一种无泄漏、固定候选者的协议，用于测量冻结的JEPA风格目标是否正确排序候选动作。在官方发布的JEPA-WM检查点上，针对导航和操作类控制任务进行审计。通过视觉骨干网络扩展、训练不足控制、匹配预算控制及度量循环性控制排除 trivial 解释，并分析闭环重规划对结果的掩盖效应。

Result: 1. 假设严重失效：在操作和迷宫领域，得分最高的候选动作几乎总是次优的，出现系统性反转。2. 鲁棒性验证：即使替换为视频预训练的V-JEPA 1/2编码器，缺陷依然存在。3. 掩盖机制发现：高频闭环重规划掩盖了初始计划排序失败的问题；降低重规划频率后，成功率急剧下降，证明闭环成功率系统性地高估了潜在表示的可排序性。

Conclusion: 冻结的JEPA潜在世界模型中的潜在空间距离不能可靠地用于动作排序。现有的闭环成功指标因重规划机制而失真，未能反映底层潜在表示的真实能力。研究强调了直接审计发布的世界模型动作可排序性的重要性，而非仅依赖闭环性能。

Abstract: Reward-free latent world models plan by scoring candidate actions with distances in a frozen latent space: an action is preferred if its predicted future embedding lands closer to the goal embedding. This silently assumes that latent closeness is action-rankable, i.e., that ordering candidates by latent distance agrees with ordering them by true cost. We audit this assumption directly. We introduce ARC-Bench, a no-leak, fixed-candidate protocol that measures whether frozen JEPA-style objectives rank candidate actions correctly, and apply it to official released JEPA-WM checkpoints across navigation and manipulation-style control. The assumption fails, severely and structurally: on the official manipulation audits the top-scored candidate is almost always suboptimal, and the same inversion appears in the maze domains. A controlled visual-backbone extension shows that the defect persists when DINOv2 is replaced by video-pretrained V-JEPA 1 and V-JEPA 2 encoders at ViT-L/ViT-G scale. Provenance, undertraining, matched-budget backbone controls, and metric-circularity controls rule out trivial explanations. We then explain why this defect has stayed invisible: closed-loop replanning masks it. When we reduce the planner's replanning frequency, success collapses in both a navigation and a manipulation domain, and the episodes rescued by frequent replanning are enriched for severe first-plan ranking failures in the PointMaze first-plan diagnostic. Closed-loop success rates therefore systematically overstate the rankability of frozen latent representations. ARC-Bench supplies the measurement, and the masking mechanism the explanation, for methods that adapt, amortize, or replan around latent-space planners without directly auditing released JEPA-WM action rankability.

</details>


### [157] [RAPID: Reliability-Aware Pair Importance Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05481)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAPID方法，通过分离可靠性门控目标与自适应配对提议，解决示例关系蒸馏中的计算效率问题，在AG News和SST-2数据集上显著提升学生模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统示例关系蒸馏因需计算所有样本对而具有二次复杂度，均匀子采样则可能低效利用有限的关系预算。

Method: 引入RAPID（Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation），将可靠性门控的师生关系从完整的自适应配对提议中分离；利用校准的教师熵和detach的学生-教师残差确定评估重点，并通过精确逆提议校正使损失和梯度估计器条件无偏。

Result: 在AG News（BERT到DistilBERT）和SST-2（DistilBERT到DistilBERT）两个文本分类任务中，可靠性门控关系蒸馏取得最高平均准确率（AG News: 94.285±0.054%, SST-2: 88.800±0.532%）；RAPID表现次之但优于交叉熵基线，验证了目标可靠性与评估优先级可分离的设计维度。

Conclusion: 可靠性门控关系蒸馏能有效提升学生模型性能，且模块化设计（分离目标可靠性与评估优先级）是优化关系蒸馏效率与效果的关键方向。

Abstract: Inter example relational distillation transfers a teacher's representation geometry by matching relations among examples within a mini batch. Computing all pairs has quadratic complexity in the batch size, whereas uniform subsampling may use a limited relation budget inefficiently. We introduce Reliability Aware Pair Importance Distillation, or RAPID, which separates a reliability gated relational target from a full support adaptive pair proposal. Reliability determines which teacher relations are emphasized, while calibrated teacher entropy and detached student-teacher residuals determine which relations are evaluated. Exact inverse proposal correction makes the loss and gradient estimators conditionally unbiased with respect to the gated mini batch target. We evaluate RAPID in two text classification settings: AG News with BERT-to-DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 256, and SST-2 with DistilBERT to DistilBERT distillation using three paired seeds and a relation budget of 64. Reliability gated relational distillation achieves the highest observed mean student accuracy on both datasets: 94.285 plus or minus 0.054 percent on AG News and 88.800 plus or minus 0.532 percent on SST-2. RAPID ranks second, achieving 94.241 plus or minus 0.025 percent and 88.685 plus or minus 0.462 percent, respectively, compared with 94.154 plus or minus 0.124 percent and 87.271 plus or minus 0.162 percent for the cross entropy baseline. Pilot evaluations are counted toward the same total budget as the main relation evaluations. Across both settings, the gated target yields the highest mean accuracy, while the adaptive proposal remains within seed-level variation. These results support the modular view that target reliability and evaluation priority are separable design dimensions.

</details>


### [158] [PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN: Prior-Guided Joint Probabilistic Forecasting of Clinical Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05488)
*Weizhi Nie,Rihao Chang,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN框架，通过联合概率预测多变量生理轨迹提升临床预测精度。在MIMIC-IV数据集上，该模型实现了最低RMSE和次低MAE，显著优于确定性TimeKAN。尽管轨迹预测性能优异，但衍生的风险评分在事件检测任务中表现弱于专用分类器，表明精确的生理预测并不直接等同于可靠的临床事件检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床恶化是通过耦合且部分观测的轨迹发生的，而非单一的诊断标签。现有方法难以捕捉这种复杂的动态过程，因此需要一种能够进行联合概率预测多变量生理轨迹的框架，以提供更可解释和准确的中间任务表示。

Method: 引入PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN，这是一种“轨迹优先”的框架。其核心组件包括：感知缺失值的时序编码器、软器官系统先验、患者特异性关系建模、非线性Kolmogorov-Arnold消息传递机制，以及基于低秩多元Student-t分布的输出头。该方法旨在结合结构信息与非线性表达能力，实现对多变量生理信号的联合概率预测。

Result: 在包含6,882名患者和54,694个时间窗口的MIMIC-IV衍生队列上进行评估。结果显示：1. 点预测方面：获得最低的RMSE (0.52656) 和第二低的归一化MAE (0.37727)，相比确定性TimeKAN MAE降低0.52%。2. 概率预测方面：边际负对数似然(NLL)为0.66380，CRPS为0.27301。3. 覆盖率方面：名义置信区间为50%、80%、95%时的实际覆盖率分别为0.533、0.831、0.958，表现出良好的校准性。4. 消融实验表明：移除关系结构导致最大的性能损失；增加协方差秩能提升联合似然但对点预测精度影响有限。

Conclusion: 联合轨迹预测提供了一个可检查的中间任务，能够显著提升生理指标预测的准确性。然而，仅凭准确的生理轨迹预测不足以构建校准良好的临床事件检测器（衍生的风险评分AUROC仅为0.603，低于专用GRU-D分类器的0.650）。研究结论指出，未来的临床声明需解决从连续轨迹预测到离散事件检测之间的映射校准问题。

Abstract: Clinical deterioration unfolds through coupled, partially observed trajectories, not a single diagnostic label. We introduce PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN, a trajectory-first framework for joint probabilistic forecasting of multivariate physiology. It combines missingness-aware temporal encoders, a soft organ-system prior, patient-specific relations, nonlinear Kolmogorov-Arnold messages, and a low-rank multivariate Student-t head. We evaluate 24-hour histories and six-hour forecasts on a frozen MIMIC-IV-derived cohort of 6,882 patients and 54,694 windows. Across five seeds and 13 models, PGP-Clinical-TimeKAN obtains the second-lowest normalized MAE (0.37727 +/- 0.00029) and the lowest RMSE (0.52656 +/- 0.00034). It reduces MAE by 0.52% relative to deterministic TimeKAN. For probabilistic forecasting, it reaches a marginal NLL of 0.66380 and a CRPS of 0.27301. Empirical coverage is 0.533, 0.831, and 0.958 for nominal 50%, 80%, and 95% intervals. Removing relational structure causes the largest ablation loss. Increasing covariance rank improves joint likelihood but has little effect on point accuracy. A trajectory-derived risk score remains weaker than a dedicated GRU-D classifier (AUROC 0.603 versus 0.650), which limits the present clinical claim. Joint trajectory forecasting therefore provides an inspectable intermediate task, but accurate physiology forecasts alone do not ensure a calibrated event detector.

</details>


### [159] [SciLitBench: Benchmark and Design Principles for LLM-Powered Systematic Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05505)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SciLitBench，一个涵盖标题筛选、全文筛选和数据提取的多阶段基准测试。研究发现，尽管LLM在筛选任务中通过明确标准可显著提升表现，但在数据提取方面存在严重的可靠性瓶颈，性能随信息复杂度急剧下降，揭示了LLM辅助证据合成中的实际能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型（LLM）评估通常孤立地检查系统综述的各个阶段，而系统综述需要跨数千条记录的持续人类判断。为了填补这一空白，需要对LLM在多阶段系统综述流程中的综合表现进行系统性评估。

Method: 构建了SciLitBench基准，包含42,981条检索记录、1,012篇全文和888篇纳入论文的标注。该基准涵盖标题/摘要筛选、全文筛选和模式引导的数据提取三个阶段。研究在来自六个模型家族的22个开源LLM上进行了评估，并分析了显式纳入/排除标准及研究者撰写的推理过程对性能的影响。

Result: 1. 标题/摘要筛选：显式的纳入和排除标准使F2分数提高了28.8%。
2. 全文筛选：研究者撰写的推理理由使性能提高了15%。
3. 数据提取：表现出不同的可靠性特征，准确率从发表年份的0.97下降到计算方法的Jaccard重叠度的0.37。最强模型仅能恢复30%的已标注评估证据和25%的限制条件。

Conclusion: SciLitBench确定了高召回率筛选与完整证据提取之间的实用界限，并为评估LLM辅助证据合成提供了一个可复现的资源。这表明当前LLM在复杂的结构化数据提取任务中仍存在显著局限。

Abstract: Systematic reviews require sustained human judgment across thousands of records, yet existing evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically examine review stages in isolation. We introduce SciLitBench, a multi-stage benchmark spanning title and abstract screening, full-text screening, and schema-guided data extraction, with 42,981 retrieved records, 1,012 full texts, and annotations for 888 included papers. Across 22 open-weight LLMs from six model families, explicit inclusion and exclusion criteria improve title and abstract screening $F_2$ by 28.8\%, while researcher-authored rationales improve full-text screening by 15\%. Data extraction reveals a different reliability regime: performance declines from 0.97 accuracy for publication year to 0.37 Jaccard overlap for computational approach, while the strongest models recover only 30\% of annotated evaluation evidence and 25\% of limitations. SciLitBench identifies a practical boundary between high-recall screening and evidence-complete extraction and provides a reproducible resource for evaluating LLM-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [160] [SCAFFOLD: Self-Improving Web Agents via Recursive Parametric Skill Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05511)
*Bowei He,Xiaokun Zhang,Meng Ding,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Scaffold框架，通过构建递归技能层次结构、压缩冗余和蒸馏模型权重，解决视觉Web代理在长视界界面中知识复用率低的问题，显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉Web代理通常孤立学习任务并丢弃已积累的程序性知识；现有的技能增强框架将技能库视为扁平或两级缓存，缺乏压缩冗余和递归组合技能的机制。

Method: 1. 在多实例抽象约束下，从成功轨迹中提取参数化可执行技能；2. 维护递归组成的技能层次结构，高层技能调用低层技能；3. 基于最小描述长度（MDL）准则和行为等价性检查压缩技能库；4. 定期将技能增强的轨迹蒸馏回模型权重以内化抽象知识。

Result: 在WebArena、VisualWebArena和Online-Mind2Web的保留集上，Scaffold的成功率比最强的技能增强基线高出11.1-17.2个绝对点，并在五次自我改进迭代中表现出单调增益且无库崩溃现象。

Conclusion: Scaffold通过递归技能组织和知识内化机制，有效提升了视觉Web代理的泛化能力和自我改进性能，解决了技能冗余和知识遗忘问题。

Abstract: Web agents need to navigate visually rich, long-horizon interfaces that change across sites, yet most previous agents still learn each task in isolation and discard the procedural knowledge they accumulate. Recent skill-augmented frameworks take an important first step, but they treat the skill library as a flat or two-tier prompt-side cache and offer no principled mechanism for compressing redundancy or composing skills recursively. We introduce \textsc{Scaffold}, a self-improving framework for visual web agents that (i) induces parametric, executable skills from successful trajectories under a multi-instance abstraction constraint, (ii) maintains a recursively composed hierarchy in which higher-level skills invoke lower-level ones, (iii) compacts the library via a minimum-description-length (MDL) criterion and behavioral equivalence checking, and (iv) periodically distills skill-augmented trajectories back into model weights to internalize the abstractions. Across WebArena, VisualWebArena, and a held-out split of Online-Mind2Web, \textsc{Scaffold} improves success rate by $11.1$--$17.2$ absolute points over the strongest skill-augmented baseline and shows monotonic gains across five self-improvement iterations without library collapse. We release the code and documents in the Github \href{https://github.com/BokwaiHo/SCAFFOLD}{repository}.

</details>


### [161] [From Version Conflicts to Decision Conflicts: Selective Revalidation for Long-Running AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08015)
*Yongjian Lyu,Yang Ren,Ruofei Lai,Wenting Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATR机制，通过记录并增量重检行动理由条件，解决长运行AI代理中的状态变更与决策冲突问题，显著优于全量扫描方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行AI代理在执行外部操作前，其依赖的状态可能已发生变化。传统乐观并发控制仅能检测版本冲突，无法判断该变化是否使原行动的合理性失效（即决策冲突），导致需重新规划或错误执行的风险。

Method: ATR（Action Justification Tracking）机制：记录支持待执行操作的显式、可执行条件；当状态变更时，仅重检受影响的关联条件以判定是否为决策冲突；根据结果保留行动、刷新非决定性元数据、要求重新规划或阻止执行；最终通过事务或比较并设置操作绑定检查后的状态以确保一致性。

Result: 在21万次受控执行和15种变异场景中，ATR准确匹配所有开发者指定的结果，无假允许或误阻断。在持久化SQLite检查点/恢复测试中，相比FullScan方法，ATR每次变更平均评估0.6个条件（vs 6.0），读取4,093次时耗时9.3微秒（vs 2595.9微秒），性能提升显著。

Conclusion: ATR实现了确定性可控的可行性验证，证明了其在处理AI代理决策冲突方面的有效性，但本研究未涉及生产环境的通用性或所需条件的自动提取。

Abstract: Long-running AI agents may read state, reason, wait for tools or human approval, and perform an external action much later. The state that justified the action can change in the meantime. For example, after an agent proposes an 80 GBP refund under a limit of 100, a customer-name change affects only presentation metadata, a new limit of 90 still permits the refund, a limit of 50 invalidates it, and a refund issued by another worker must prevent a duplicate. Standard optimistic concurrency control and version checks can detect that previously read state has changed, but by themselves do not determine whether that change invalidates the pending action's justification. We call any detected version change a version conflict; when that change invalidates the action's justification, it is also a decision conflict. ATR records the explicit, executable conditions that justify a pending action and rechecks only the conditions affected by a change before releasing the external operation. It can retain the action, refresh non-decisive metadata, require replanning, or block execution; a target-side transaction or compare-and-set binds checked state to commit. Across 210,000 controlled executions over 15 mutation cases, ATR matched every developer-specified outcome with no false allows or blocks. In ten durable SQLite checkpoint/resume cells, it evaluated 0.6 conditions per change versus 6.0 for FullScan. At 4,093 recorded reads, ATR took 9.3 microseconds versus 2595.9 microseconds for FullScan. These deterministic results establish controlled feasibility, not production generality or automatic extraction of the required conditions.

</details>


### [162] [Reasoning-Aware Compression: Identifying and Protecting Vulnerable Reasoning Circuits for Energy-Efficient LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05512)
*Leonard Twagirayezu,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种感知推理的压缩框架，通过识别并保留关键模块的FP16精度，解决了统一量化导致的推理链延长和能耗增加问题，在保持精度的同时实现了帕累托最优的能量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）部署时能耗巨大，而现有的统一量化方法可能破坏关键的推理电路，且未考虑量化对推理过程本身的影响。

Method: 构建推理感知压缩框架：在五个基准测试上进行硬件级GPU能量测量；通过对校准集进行扰动扫描，分析196-224个（层，投影）对的INT4脆弱性；选择性地将最敏感的电路恢复为FP16精度。

Result: 发现INT4量化可能因延长推理链导致净能耗增加；量化脆弱性具有任务依赖性（如数学推理中注意力投影更关键）；R1-Qwen-7B在ProofWriter上实现Top-10%排名，相比FP16精度提升12个百分点，同时降低9.7%能耗，且在五个基准测试中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 选择性压缩优于统一量化方法，能够在不牺牲推理性能的前提下显著优化能源效率，揭示了推理过程中不同模块对量化的敏感度差异。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) impose substantial energy costs during deployment, yet current compression methods apply uniform quantization across all components, risking damage to critical reasoning circuits. We present a reasoning-aware compression framework that benchmarks quantization conditions across five reasoning benchmarks, GSM8K, FOLIO, MATH-500, ProofWriter, and MuSiQue, with hardware-level GPU energy measurement; profiles per-module INT4 vulnerability across all 196-224 (layer, projection) pairs via a perturbation sweep on a held-out calibration split, then selectively restores the most sensitive circuits to FP16. Three findings emerge. First, INT4 quantization can increase energy by extending reasoning chains; a 25% power reduction becomes a net energy increase on GSM8K. Second, vulnerability is task-dependent: attention projections are more critical for mathematical reasoning, and sensitivity patterns differ by architecture in logical inference. Third, selective compression achieves Pareto-optimal points inaccessible to uniform methods: R1-Qwen-7B Top-10% on ProofWriter gains +12 pp over FP16 at -9.7% energy, validated on held-out data across five reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [When and What to Teach: Budget-Aware Online Adaptation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05513)
*Jianwei Zhang,Sihan Cao,Pengcheng Zheng,Ya Wen,Pei Ke,Kuien Liu,Shen Gao,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种预算感知的在线教学框架，通过“何时”查询教师模型和“保留哪些”交互轮次来优化轻量化本地模型的部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实环境中部署Web代理需要持续在线适应，但使用强大的专有模型商业成本过高，而依赖轻量级本地模型又面临标准交互式反馈带来的高昂计算成本问题。传统轨迹级偏好优化浪费了预算在无法解决的回合和冗余执行上。

Method: 提出了“基于分数的预算化轨迹修剪在线教学”（Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming）框架。该框架包含两个核心机制：1. 可解性感知教师门控（Solvability-aware teacher gate），决定何时查询教师模型；2. 分数引导的轮次选择机制（Score-guided turn selection），决定保留哪些信息丰富的交互轮次。

Result: 在MiniWoB和TimeWarp基准测试中，该方法在保持首次通过率相当的情况下，平均减少了22.6%的教师调用次数和52.1%的学生训练计算量。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了在线教学中预算浪费的问题，实现了高效、低成本的轻量化Web代理模型自适应优化。

Abstract: Web agents have achieved significant success in automating complex internet tasks but deploying them in real-world environments requires continuous online adaptation. Given that deploying powerful proprietary models remains commercially cost-prohibitive, practitioners must rely on lightweight local models that evolve post-deployment via online teaching from a stronger teacher. However, standard interactive feedback imposes prohibitive costs. We show that conventional trajectory-level preference optimization wastes budget on both unresolvable episodes and redundant execution turns. To resolve these inefficiencies, we propose \textbf{Score-Guided Online Teaching with Budgeted Trajectory Trimming}, a budget-aware framework that systematically orchestrates \textbf{when} and \textbf{what} to teach. Specifically, our framework integrates a solvability-aware teacher gate to dictate \textbf{when} to query the teacher model and a score-guided turn selection mechanism to decide \textbf{what} informative turns to retain. Extensive experiments on MiniWoB and TimeWarp demonstrate that our method achieves comparable first-pass success while reducing teacher calls by 22.6\% and student training compute by 52.1\% on average. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjw131f1fc/budgeted-online-teaching.

</details>


### [164] [The Failure Happens Before the Drift: The Social Dynamics of Values in LLM Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05514)
*Farah Atif,Sougata Saha,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了基于LLM的代理在模拟人类价值观方面的忠实度。结果显示，超过50%的代理初始即无法准确表达分配的WVS价值观，且存在系统性偏差。尽管模拟对话具有语义多样性，但风格重复，表明当前LLM代理作为长期保存和代表多样化人类价值观的代理仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型（LLM）的代理越来越多地作为社会科学研究中人类参与者的替代者，目前尚不清楚它们能否忠实地模拟多样且相互冲突的人类价值体系。

Method: 构建了一个基于世界价值观调查（WVS）的模拟框架，让具有不同沟通风格的文化多样代理进行纵向、富含价值的讨论。在约4,000次对话中，涉及1,200个人格、15个主题以及三个模型（GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Flash, Gemma-4-E4B），评估了价值忠实度、价值漂移和对话真实性。

Result: 1. 超过50%的人格从一开始就无法表达其分配的WVS特征；2-7%的人格在重复对话后发生漂移。2. 移除人口统计细节虽能提高某些模型的忠实度，但未改变整体趋势：模拟的价值分布仍系统性地偏离分配的WVS特征。3. 与人类讨论相比，模拟对话在风格一致性与语义多样性之间呈现不同的权衡，通常产生内容多样但风格重复的交流。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM代理能够生成看似合理的对话，但作为代表和长期保留多样化人类价值档案的代理，其能力仍然有限。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used as proxies for human participants in social science research, yet it remains unclear whether they can faithfully simulate diverse and conflicting human value systems. We present a World Values Survey (WVS)-grounded simulation framework where culturally diverse agents with different communication styles engage in longitudinal, value-laden discussions. Across approximately 4,000 conversations involving 1,200 personas, 15 topics, and three models (GPT-4o, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B), we evaluate value faithfulness, value drift, and conversational realism. We find that more than 50\% of personas fail to express their assigned WVS profiles from the outset, while 2-7\% drift after repeated conversations. Ablations removing demographic details improve faithfulness for some models but do not change the broader trend: simulated value distributions still systematically deviate from the assigned WVS profiles. Compared to human discussions, simulated dialogues show a different trade-off between stylistic consistency and semantic diversity, often producing content-wise varied but stylistically repetitive exchanges. These findings suggest that current LLM agents can generate plausible conversations, but remain limited proxies for representing and preserving diverse human value profiles over time.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond "AI Helps Humans": Decision-Targeted Evaluation Design for Human-Agent Teams in the Agentic Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05527)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TEAM-Design规则，在固定重放预算下优化人机协作工作流的部署决策，通过为每种任务分配两个重放概率（分别对应“纯人工”和“纯AI”基线），优先对难以预测且比较困难的任务进行重放，以验证工作流是否优于单一模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作工作流部署中，需判断其是否优于纯人工或纯AI方案。但部署后无法直接观察替代方案结果，重放成本高昂。现有方法（如基准测试、方差采样、贝叶斯信息法）未直接针对此特定决策问题，缺乏有效指导哪些任务应优先进行哪种基线的重评估。

Method: 提出TEAM-Design规则：为每个任务设定两个重放概率，分别对应两种基线对比。该规则提高那些基线结果难以从已知信息预测且对比难度大的任务的重放概率，降低重放昂贵任务的概率。通过理论证明其在预算约束下的最优性及错误声明控制能力，并在临床场景和代码基准中进行实证分析。

Result: 1. 理论证明：该规则解决了预算受限的设计问题，随机抽样仍能有效控制错误判定风险。2. 实证分析：重新分析6个临床场景（无一显示人机协作优于两者）和1个代码基准（显示优于两者）。3. 性能表现：当两种对比难度差异明显时效果最佳；若难度相当，可能劣于基于方差的分配策略。

Conclusion: TEAM-Design提供了一种针对人机协作工作流部署评估的优化框架，特别适用于两种基线对比难度不对称的场景，为资源受限下的决策提供了理论依据和实践工具。

Abstract: Wherever a coding agent works under engineer supervision, or a clinical model assists a radiologist, the deployment question is whether to keep the human-AI workflow or replace it with the human alone or the agent alone. The human-AI workflow is worth keeping only if it beats both of those alternatives. Yet once it is deployed, neither alternative outcome is observed: recovering one means replaying the task under that alternative, and every replay costs expert time or compute. Under a fixed replay budget, the design question is therefore which tasks should be more likely to receive a human-only replay, and which an agent-only replay. Existing methods do not directly target this decision. Agent benchmarks do not choose which missing baseline to measure, variance-based sampling ignores which of the two comparisons is closer to failing, and Bayesian information methods focus on learning model parameters instead of making the deployment decision. We propose TEAM-Design, a rule that gives every task two replay probabilities, one per baseline. It raises a probability where the missing baseline outcome is hard to predict from what is already known about the task and where that comparison is harder to establish, and lowers it where replay is expensive. We prove that the rule solves this budgeted design problem, and that drawing the replays at random from recorded probabilities still controls the chance of wrongly declaring that the workflow beats both. We reanalyze 6 clinical settings, where no human-AI workflow beats both alternatives, and a coding benchmark, where one does, then evaluate TEAM-Design on synthetic designs and on a semi-synthetic design built from a real chest X-ray reader study. TEAM-Design works best when one of the two comparisons is clearly harder to settle than the other, and can do worse than variance-based allocation when the two are similarly difficult.

</details>


### [166] [EdgeMem: LLM-Free Agent Memory Construction and Retrieval via Evidence-Preserving Multi-Anchor Hypergraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05553)
*Zeyang Cui,Jiannong Cao,Zhiyuan Wen,Bo Yuan,Junlan Feng,Shengyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EdgeMem，一种基于多锚点超图的非生成式代理记忆方法，通过保留原始交互内容并利用轻量级线索进行组织，实现高效、高保真的信息检索与问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理记忆方法通常将交互历史压缩为LLM生成的摘要或表示，这增加了计算成本，且可能在系统知晓未来查询需求前就丢失关键细节。

Method: 构建由轻量级局部处理生成的多锚点超图（multi-anchor hypergraph），通过互补内容、时间和情景线索组织原始交互轮次；检索直接返回源证据，仅在使用最终答案生成时调用LLM。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准测试中表现优异；在LoCoMo上，使用共享提示词的情况下，其严格评判得分（61.01）高于其他六种复现系统（最高58.70），且记忆构建与检索无需生成式LLM调用。

Conclusion: 保留并组织源证据而非依赖生成式记忆管理，能为代理记忆提供有效且高效的基础。

Abstract: Agent memory allows LLM agents to use earlier interactions when answering new queries. Existing methods often compress interaction histories into summaries or other LLM-generated representations. Repeated generation adds cost and can discard answer-bearing details before the system knows what a future query will require. We propose EdgeMem, an agent-memory method built around a simple principle: preserve original interaction turns and organize them through complementary content, temporal, and episodic cues. EdgeMem realizes this principle with a multi-anchor hypergraph constructed by lightweight local processing. Retrieval directly returns source evidence and reserves LLM use for final answer generation, combining structured access to multi-session histories with faithful retention of the original conversation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show strong retrieval and memory-grounded question answering; on LoCoMo, EdgeMem achieves the highest strict-judge score among seven reproduced systems under a shared prompt (61.01 versus 58.70), while construction and retrieval require no generative-LLM calls. Overall, EdgeMem shows that preserving and organizing source evidence provides an effective and efficient foundation for agent memory without generative memory management.

</details>


### [167] [Deep belief networks are exact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05572)
*Gleb Smirnov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 证明了任何严格正的概率分布都可以由具有有限参数的Sigmoid信念网络精确表示，从而回答了Sutskever和Hinton提出的一个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Sutskever和Hinton关于Sigmoid信念网络表达能力的问题，特别是探讨其是否能精确表示任意严格正的概率分布，而不仅仅是近似。

Method: 将Sutskever和Hinton提出的概率共享近似方法升级为精确表示，核心证明工具是布劳威尔不动点定理（Brouwer's fixed-point theorem）。

Result: 证明了对于定义在{-1,1}^n上的任何严格正概率分布，都存在一个具有有限参数的Sigmoid信念网络对其进行精确表示。

Conclusion: Sigmoid信念网络具有强大的表达能力，能够精确表示所有严格正的二元变量联合分布，这一结论完善了对其理论性质的理解。

Abstract: We prove that every strictly positive probability distribution on \(\{-1,1\}^n\) is represented exactly by a sigmoid belief network with finite parameters. This answers a question of Sutskever and Hinton. The proof upgrades their probability-sharing approximation to exact representation using Brouwer's fixed-point theorem.

</details>


### [168] [EnvCraft: Synthesizing Executable Environments in Agentic RL for Claw-like Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05576)
*Yirong Zeng,Shen You,Jinhang Feng,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Yutai Hou,Hao Cong,Yuxian Wang,Wu Ning,Wang Xu,Bibo Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EnvCraft框架，通过自动化合成可执行沙盒环境和拓扑感知数据生成，解决Agentic RL训练环境稀缺问题，在Qwen3/3.5模型上显著提升Claw-style及通用工具使用基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从被动接口转向自主Claw-like智能体，但Agentic RL扩展受限于交互式训练环境的严重稀缺；现有合成环境仅限工具调用端点，无法满足端到端真实世界需求。

Method: 引入EnvCraft自动化框架：1) 环境合成引擎构建沙盒隔离工作空间；2) 拓扑感知数据生成引擎产生连贯的任务轨迹。最终合成139个交互环境约20K复杂任务用于Agentic RL训练。

Result: 在Qwen3/3.5 (8B-32B)模型上实验显示：Claw-style基准提升+11.9%，通用工具使用基准提升+8.0%，同时推理Token成本降低。

Conclusion: 合成的可执行环境为训练提供了鲁棒且泛化的学习信号，有效弥合了现有环境与真实需求间的差距。

Abstract: The paradigm of LLMs has rapidly shifted from passive language interfaces to autonomous Claw-like agents that execute long-horizon tasks across stateful workspaces. While Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) provides a promising path to optimize these agents, its scaling is heavily bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of interactive training environments. Existing synthetic environments are strictly limited to tool-calling endpoints, rendering them insufficient for accommodating the end-to-end real-world demands of claw-like agents. To bridge this gap, we introduce EnvCraft, an automated framework for synthesizing executable environments and scalable training data. Specifically, EnvCraft employs an environment synthesis engine to build sandbox-isolated workspaces, alongside a topology-aware data generation engine to produce coherent task trajectories. Overall, we synthesize 139 interactive environments comprising approximately 20K complex tasks for Agentic RL training. Experiments on Qwen3/3.5 models (8B-32B) show that our method yields gains of up to +11.9% on Claw-style benchmarks and +8.0% on general tool-use benchmarks, with concurrent reductions in inference token cost. The results confirm that synthesized executable environments provide robust and generalizable learning signals for training.

</details>


### [169] [Planning and Scheduling Business Processes under Control-Flow Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05578)
*Michel Kunkler,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对业务流程中因执行路径不确定性导致的调度难题，本文提出两种机会约束优化方法：分阶段方法（兼顾可行性与工期）和集成方法。评估显示集成方法工期更优但扩展性差，分阶段方法在大规模场景下更具可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程活动调度旨在提高效率（如缩短完工时间），但由于执行过程中基于数据的决策导致确切的活动序列具有不确定性，使得调度极具挑战性。利用历史日志中的概率信息可预判成功路径，从而优化计划可行性及减少冗余活动。

Method: 将问题建模为机会约束优化问题，并提出两种公式化方法：1) 分解方法：分为两阶段，规划阶段在满足可行性约束下最小化预期冗余活动数，调度阶段对已规划活动最小化工期；2) 集成方法：将规划与调度合并为单一公式进行优化。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集和一个合成数据集上的评估表明，集成方法能产生更优的完工时间（makespan），但在大规模数据下计算不可行；相比之下，分解方法能够扩展到大规模设置。

Conclusion: 在处理具有不确定性的业务流程调度时，虽然集成优化能获得更好的性能指标，但分解方法在平衡计算效率与大规模应用需求方面表现更佳，提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Scheduling activities in business processes can improve efficiency (e.g., reduce makespan), but is challenging because the exact sequence of activities required to complete a case is often uncertain due to decisions based on data that emerges during execution. Nevertheless, probabilistic information regarding such decisions can often be estimated or derived from historical execution logs, and can help anticipate which execution paths are likely to lead to successful completion. Planning with particular execution paths affects feasibility, i.e., the probability of successful completion, and the expected number of superfluous activities that are planned but never executed. We frame the problem as a chance-constrained optimization problem and present two formulations: A decomposed approach with two stages, a planning stage that minimizes the expected number of superfluous activities subject to a feasibility constraint, and a scheduling stage that minimizes the makespan over the planned activities; and an integrated approach that combines planning and scheduling into a single formulation. Evaluation on two real-world and one synthetic dataset shows that the integrated approach yields superior makespans but is intractable at scale, while the decomposed approach scales to large settings.

</details>


### [170] [Agents Trust Tools Too Much: Measuring Reliance on Unreliable Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05587)
*Hoyeol Yang,Woojung Song,Taewon Kim,Jonghyun Song,Seoyeon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在工具使用中存在严重的“过度信任”问题，即使工具返回信息错误，代理仍倾向于采纳。现有干预措施无法一致性地解决此问题，表明需要新的评估和干预方法来增强代理对工具输出的验证能力及冲突透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具使用代理评估主要关注任务完成能力，并假设工具返回的信息是可靠的。然而，现实世界中的工具可能返回看似合理但实际错误的信息。本研究旨在调查代理如何应对不可靠的工具返回，识别由此产生的失败模式。

Method: 对14个LLM进行评估，使用三种工具：网络搜索、LLM子代理委托和代码执行。通过人为污染各工具的返回数据，测量代理是否在最终答案中采纳了这些被污染的内容。同时分析推理轨迹以理解代理的内部处理过程。此外，尝试从用户提示、工具提供商元数据和代理后训练三个层面进行干预，以缓解过度信任现象。

Result: 代理在所有三种设置下均表现出高水平的过度信任：每种工具的平均采纳率超过三分之一，其中网络搜索高达68.0%。分析发现一种令人担忧的失败模式：代理往往能内部识别冲突甚至恢复正确答案，但最终仅呈现错误答案且未向用户发出警告。尽管部分干预措施对特定模型或工具有效，但没有一种能跨工具一致地缓解过度信任。

Conclusion: 对不可靠工具的过度信任是一个严重且持续的失败模式。未来的研究和实践应致力于开发能够验证工具输出并在遇到未决冲突时透明沟通的评估框架与干预机制。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of tool-using agents primarily measure whether an agent can successfully complete diverse tasks with tools. These evaluations generally assume that tools return reliable information. However, tool returns in real-world systems can be plausible yet incorrect. We investigate how agents respond to unreliable tool returns by evaluating fourteen LLMs using three tools-web search, LLM sub-agent delegation, and code execution. For each tool, we corrupt its returns and measure whether agents adopt the corrupted content in their final answers. Agents exhibit high levels of overtrust across all three settings: the mean adoption rate exceeds one third for every tool and reaches 68.0% for web search. Analysis of reasoning traces reveals a particularly concerning failure mode: agents often recognize conflicts and even recover the correct answer internally, yet present only the corrupted answer without warning the user. To mitigate agents' overtrust in tool returns, we intervene at three levels: prompting by the user, metadata from the tool provider, and post-training by the agent builder. Although some interventions help for particular models or tools, none consistently mitigates overtrust across tools. These findings identify overtrust in unreliable tools as a serious and persistent failure mode, motivating evaluations and interventions that enable agents to validate tool outputs and transparently communicate unresolved conflicts.

</details>


### [171] [The convergent laboratory: when AI reasoning, autonomous experiments, high performance and quantum computing reshape chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05643)
*Eliu Huerta,Xiaoyun Wang,Geetika Gupta,Edward H. Sargent,Cameron J. Owen,Victor Fung,Abhishek Mitra,Austin Cheng,Emma Bouchard,Shams Mehdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TPC26会议综述：探讨AI、自主代理、自驱动实验室及量子计算在材料科学发现中的融合与变革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应学术界、国家实验室及工业界领袖对重塑材料科学发现的迫切需求，捕捉全球前沿研究的本质，并为即将到来的变革性进展提供参考。

Method: 基于TPC26会议的参与者（来自学术、国家和行业领域）的第一手经验，分析人工智能推理、自主智能体框架、自驱动实验室和容错量子处理器这四项技术的收敛效应。

Result: 揭示了上述技术同时成熟如何放大其在材料科学发现中的个体影响力，标志着化学科学领域正处于 transformative advances（变革性进步）和 productive disruption（生产性颠覆）的关键转折点。

Conclusion: 该评论作为一份参考文件，指出材料科学发现正迎来由多技术融合驱动的转型期，强调了这一全球性努力的重要性及未来潜力。

Abstract: This Comment emerges from TPC26 (https://tpc26.org), a conference convening leaders from academia, national laboratories, and industry who are reshaping materials science discovery. The meeting explored how AI, autonomous agents, self-driving labs, higher performance and quantum computing converge to amplify their individual impact on materials science discovery. The perspectives here reflect the firsthand experiences of researchers at these frontiers and capture the essence of this global endeavor. As AI-driven reasoning, autonomous agentic frameworks, self-driving laboratories, and fault-tolerant quantum processors mature simultaneously, we offer this Comment as a reference at what we believe is a tipping point of transformative advances and productive disruption in the chemical sciences.

</details>


### [172] [What LLM Trading Agents Actually Do in Production: A Six-Month, Population-Scale Record from Two Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05663)
*T. J. Barton,Chris Constantakis,Patti Hauseman,Annie Mous,Alaska Hoffman,Brian Bergeron,Hunter Goodreau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于DX Terminal Pro和DXAP真实生产环境六个月、750万次调用的大规模实证研究，揭示了自主语言模型交易代理在行为决定因素、头寸规模、收益捕获及方向性优势方面的关键发现：操作层设定主导行为，代理对波动率不敏感且缺乏上行收益捕获能力，且整体未表现出显著的方向性交易优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估自主语言模型交易代理在真实生产环境中的实际表现、行为模式及其局限性，通过大规模连续数据记录提供关于AI代理在金融市场中运作机制的实证证据。

Method: 分析两个具有相同设计谱系的系统（DX Terminal Pro和DXAP live alpha fleet）长达六个月的生产数据；涵盖3,505个用户资助的金库和500-599个用户创建的代理，涉及750万次单模型调用、30万次链上操作及23万多次多工具交互；采用回归分析、断点回归、配对重放联赛等方法进行统计验证。

Result: 1. 操作层设置比策略文本更决定行为（风险滑块解释杠杆变化，排行榜边界导致选择偏差）；2. 头寸规模对波动率不敏感（中位数杠杆恒定为5x），少数单元格持有大部分清算风险；3. 代理几乎无法捕获已实现的上行收益（43.2%的头寸有正向波动但近半数最终亏损，机械止损可挽回部分损失）；4. DXAP舰队无方向性优势，盈利能力和胜率落后于基准，不同模型家族在决策稳定性上差异显著。

Conclusion: 自主语言模型交易代理在当前技术阶段并未展现出可持续的方向性市场优势，其交易行为主要受系统配置而非策略内容驱动，且存在严重的收益捕获失效问题；研究强调方法论严谨性，并提出了包含自我纠错机制的17条方法学准则。

Abstract: We present a continuous, population-scale measurement record of autonomous language-model trading agents operating in production across two systems with one design lineage: DX Terminal Pro (3,505 user-funded vaults trading real ETH in Base memecoin markets for 21 days, February to March 2026) and the DXAP live alpha fleet (500 to 599 user-created agents all-history, 91 to 117 concurrently active, trading Hyperliquid perpetuals, June to August 2026). The record spans roughly six months, 7.5M single-model invocations with about 300K onchain actions, and a further 231,638 multi-tool turns producing 14,596 fills. Four findings carry the paper. First, the operating layer determines behavior more than anything written in strategy text: a risk slider explains leverage (+0.425 per level), agent fixed effects absorb 60% of variance, and a leaderboard render boundary causally routes selection (regression discontinuity 1.75x at the top-3 cut). Second, sizing is volatility-blind: median leverage is 5.0x in every volatility sextile, and one posture-slider cell (11% of the book) holds 62% of liquidations. Third, agents capture almost none of the upside they reach: 43.2% of positions saw at least +300 bps of favorable excursion within 24h, yet 49.3% of those closed with a negative trade return; a mechanical bracket recovers +39.0 bps per position. Fourth, neither fleet shows a directional edge. The DXAP fleet is not profitable and trails a matched Hyperliquid retail benchmark (41% vs. 50% roundtrip win rate). A paired-replay league of frontier models on 416 captured production scenarios finds decision quality statistically indistinguishable at this horizon, while choice stability differs sharply across model families. Every headline survives day-clustered inference, permutation nulls, and a common-fee restatement; the paper closes with a 17-rule methodology canon bought with our own retractions.

</details>


### [173] [CUSP: Decomposable Collective Uncertainty for Multi-Agent Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05708)
*Chung-En Johnny Yu,David Garcia,Brian Jalaian,Nathaniel D. Bastian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CUSP框架，通过语义意见池化量化多VLM系统的集体不确定性和模型冲突，无需训练即可提升系统级可靠性评估与预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法通过单个模型置信度或聚合答案的置信度来准确衡量多模态推理系统的系统级可靠性。

Method: 构建无训练不确定性量化框架CUSP：将多个VLM响应映射到共享语义空间，聚合成“语义意见”，并计算集体不确定性（分散度）和Jensen-Shannon散度（冲突度）。

Result: 在小型模型集合中，集体不确定性是最佳信号；在商业模型中，JSD表现最强；聚合预测精度比单模型提高5.6-13.0点；在多步多智能体系统中，子代理集体不确定性能有效识别系统故障并提供最佳弃权排序。

Conclusion: CUSP通过分离总分散度与模型冲突，为开放权重和商业VLM提供了有效的系统级不确定性量化手段，显著优于多数投票等基线方法。

Abstract: Aggregating heterogeneous vision-language models (VLMs) can improve multimodal reasoning, but neither an individual model's confidence nor that of the aggregated answer measures reliability at the system level. We present CUSP (Collective Uncertainty through Semantic Opinion Pooling), a training-free uncertainty quantification framework that maps multiple VLM responses to a shared semantic response space, pools them into a pooled semantic opinion, and reports two complementary system-level signals: collective uncertainty, the dispersion of the pooled opinion, and Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), the conflict among the model-level opinions. Within this pooled semantic opinion, the unnormalized collective entropy decomposes exactly into the mean of the models' individual semantic entropies and the JSD, separating total dispersion from model conflict. Requiring neither token logits nor calibration labels, CUSP applies to open-weight and commercial VLMs alike. In static multi-VLM ensembles, collective uncertainty is the strongest signal in the small-model regime (0.764 AUROC for prediction-error detection, 0.889 AUARC for abstention), outperforming uncertainty baselines majority voting and naive selection by 4.7 to 15.8 points and widening its margin as the ensemble grows; JSD is strongest in the evaluated commercial regime (0.819 AUROC, 0.910 AUARC) and ranks hard-answer model conflict with AUROC up to 0.982. The pooled prediction also improves accuracy over the average single model by 5.6 to 13.0 points. Over the full trajectory of a multi-step, multi-agent system, subagent collective uncertainty ranks system failures above chance (0.619 AUROC) and gives the best abstention ordering among the evaluated signals (0.699 AUARC).

</details>


### [174] [Recovering Temporal and Geographic Signals from Language Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05721)
*Esteban Feuerstein,Victoria Klimkowski,Juan Manuel Ortiz de Zarate,Federico Hernán Suaiter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于投影的黑盒方法，通过分析模型输出嵌入向量中的时间性和地理性信号，证明现代语言模型嵌入中编码了时空结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型嵌入是否编码了结构化的现实世界信息（特别是时间和空间维度），这对于表示分析和信息检索至关重要。

Method: 采用简单的基于投影的方法，直接作用于输出嵌入。给定少量种子示例，在嵌入空间中定义一个轴，并根据文本或实体在该轴上的投影进行排序。该方法完全黑盒且与模型无关，仅需嵌入向量，无需访问模型权重、内部激活、辅助探针或额外训练。

Result: 在时间和地理数据集上应用该方法，发现嵌入投影能够恢复有意义的 chronological（时间顺序）和 spatial（空间）结构。结果提供了证据，表明输出嵌入编码了与时空相关的信号。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅证实了语言模型嵌入中包含时空信号，还为可解释性以及下游的时空信息检索任务（如时间排序、地理排名和标记）提供了一种轻量级的实用工具。

Abstract: Understanding whether language-model embeddings encode structured real-world information is important for both representation analysis and information retrieval. We study this question for temporal and geographic signals using a simple projection-based method that operates directly on output embeddings. Given a small set of seed examples, the method defines an axis in embedding space and ranks texts or entities by their projection onto that axis. Our approach is fully black-box and model-agnostic: it requires only embeddings, without access to model weights, internal activations, auxiliary probes, or additional training. This makes it applicable to modern embedding models available only through APIs and provides a lightweight way to analyze whether temporal and spatial dimensions are present in their representation spaces. We apply the method to temporal and geographic datasets and find that embedding projections recover meaningful chronological and spatial structure. These results provide evidence that output embeddings encode signals relevant to time and space, while also offering a practical tool for interpretability and for downstream temporal and geographic information retrieval tasks, such as temporal ordering, geographic ranking, and tagging.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Prompts: Measuring and Optimizing LLM Tool-Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05736)
*Cen,Zhao,Haibo Ruan,Wenjie Chen,Pei-fen Tu,Usman Abbasi,Joel Hesch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对固定大模型的工具智能体（Tool Agents）运行时框架优化协议，无需重新训练即可通过调整提示词和中间件提升性能。实验表明该方法在多个基准测试中显著提升了准确率，并强调了评估框架可靠性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在不重新训练大型语言模型（LLM）的前提下，如何通过优化运行时环境（如提示工程、工具接口、中间件等）来提升多轮对话中工具智能体的性能问题，特别是如何在资源受限的情况下选择最优的框架配置。

Method: 提出了一个与优化器无关的协议，用于评估固定模型多轮工具智能体的运行时框架。研究范围限定为提示词和工具边界中间件的编辑（作为受保护的拦截点）。实例化了包括PRISM在内的多种优化器，其中PRISM通过聚类失败案例，并在帕累托搜索中将修复路由到提示词、工具边界中间件或联合编辑表面。评估指标包括平均保持外增益（mean held-out lift）、最坏情况增益、可重复性、成本诊断以及RelLift95（保守估计的保持外增益）。

Result: 在BFCL多轮、tau2-Retail和tau2-Telecom三个基准测试上，PRISM分别获得了14.2、14.9和10.1个百分点的平均保持外增益，且在所有基准上均表现出正的实证RelLift95。消融实验表明，这些提升主要归功于故障表面路由和编辑模式约束。

Conclusion: 仅报告平均保持外增益是不够的，因为某些搜索过程虽然能找到大幅增益，但可能导致脆弱的更新。因此，在报告优化效果时，必须同时报告所选框架的可靠性指标（如RelLift95），以确保系统的稳健性。

Abstract: LLM tool agents can be improved without retraining by modifying the runtime harness around a fixed model: prompts, tool interfaces, middleware, state handling, and recovery logic. We study this setting as resource-bounded harness selection for fixed-model multi-turn tool agents, with the search surface scoped to prompts and tool-boundary middleware: edits are guarded intercepts at the tool boundary, not arbitrary rewriting of agent execution logic. Our optimizer-agnostic protocol reports mean held-out lift, worst-condition lift, repeatability, logged cost diagnostics, and RelLift95(B), a conservative estimate of the held-out gain of the harness selected under budget B. We instantiate the protocol with prompt-only and prompt-plus-middleware optimizers, including PRISM, which clusters failures and routes repairs to prompt, tool-boundary middleware, or joint edit surfaces within a Pareto search. On BFCL multi-round, tau2-Retail, and tau2-Telecom, PRISM obtains mean held-out lifts of 14.2, 14.9, and 10.1 percentage points and positive empirical RelLift95 on all three benchmarks, and a component ablation attributes the margin chiefly to failure-surface routing and the edit-pattern constraint. Across optimizers, the results show that some search procedures can occasionally find large gains but still choose brittle updates, so the reliability of the chosen harness should be reported alongside average held-out lift.

</details>


### [176] [The Normalization of Deviance in AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05749)
*Emilio Barkett,Alexander Kimpton,Daniel Graham,Yusuf Kundgol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI风险研究过度关注能力风险，忽视了组织层面的结构性失效风险。通过类比挑战者号、三里岛和波音737 MAX事故，揭示AI开发机构存在类似的结构动力学机制，可能导致合规下的灾难性后果，呼吁在危机发生前干预这些动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险研究主要聚焦于系统能力（如过于强大、自主或价值观不对齐），而严重忽视了构建这些系统的组织机构本身是否存在导致失败的结构性倾向。

Method: 基于挑战者号航天飞机、三里岛核事故和波音737 MAX坠机事故的案例研究，识别导致这些重大技术灾难的共同结构机制，并将其映射到当代AI开发情境中。

Result: 发现现有的安全基础设施提供的保护可能少于预期；即使组织完全合规地执行了所有安全流程，仍可能产生灾难性结果。

Conclusion: AI发展的“灾前时期”仍在进行中，本文旨在使这些潜在的组织失效动态变得清晰可辨，以便在当前阶段仍能对其进行干预和中断。

Abstract: Work on the risks of artificial intelligence has focused predominantly on capability risk: the danger that systems become too powerful, too autonomous, or too misaligned with human values. Far less attention has been paid to the organizational level---to whether the institutions building these systems are themselves predisposed to drift toward failure. This paper argues that they are. Regardless of how capable AI systems become, the organizations building them face the same structural dynamics that preceded past major technological disasters. Drawing on case studies of the Space Shuttle Challenger, the Three Mile Island accident, and the Boeing 737 MAX crashes, this paper identifies the common structural mechanisms preceding each failure and maps them onto contemporary AI development. The findings suggest that existing safety infrastructure may provide less protection than it appears, as organizations can complete safety processes in full compliance and still produce catastrophic outcomes. The pre-disaster period of AI development is still underway; the purpose of this paper is to make these dynamics legible while they can still be interrupted.

</details>


### [177] [From Monolithic Blending to Agentic Orchestration: Dynamic Response for Conversational Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05758)
*Cen,Zhao,Peng Wang,Chuan Shi,Yufeng Zhang,Ying Lyu,Wanmeng Ren,Robert Xue,Claire Na Cheng,Yashar Mehdad*

Main category: cs.AI
